wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_s [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_c [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_s[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign psum_c[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // Output CSA form, no final add here\n         assign c_bot_s[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_s[N][j];\n         assign c_bot_c[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_c[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north_s (psum_s[i][j]),\n               .psum_north_c (psum_c[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south_s (psum_s[i+1][j]),\n               .psum_south_c (psum_c[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACCW_INT = 19;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Array outputs CSA form (sum and carry)\n   wire [N*ACCW_INT-1:0] c_bot_s;\n   wire [N*ACCW_INT-1:0] c_bot_c;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW_INT)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left),\n      .c_bot_s(c_bot_s), .c_bot_c(c_bot_c));\n\n   // Deskew CSA form, final add at output\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACCW_INT-1:0] cin_s = c_bot_s[j*ACCW_INT +: ACCW_INT];\n         wire [ACCW_INT-1:0] cin_c = c_bot_c[j*ACCW_INT +: ACCW_INT];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            wire [ACCW_INT-1:0] result = cin_s + cin_c;\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n               {{(ACCW-ACCW_INT){result[ACCW_INT-1]}}, result};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [ACCW_INT-1:0] dr_s [0:D-1];\n            reg [ACCW_INT-1:0] dr_c [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr_s[0] <= cin_s;\n                  dr_c[0] <= cin_c;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) begin\n                    dr_s[k] <= dr_s[k-1];\n                    dr_c[k] <= dr_c[k-1];\n dout_c;\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n               {{(ACCW-ACCW_INT){result[ACCW_INT-1]}}, result};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always ## Summary

### Best Design
`design_csa_split.v` — **2.84M area_delay_product** (delay 660ps, area 4303), down from the starting point's 4.62M (38% improvement).

### What Worked Best (in order of impact)

1. **Carry-save accumulator (CSA) replacing ripple chain** — the single biggest win. Instead of `psum_south = psum_north + prod` (a 19-bit ripple add per PE, chaining 8 deep), each PE maintains redundant (sum, carry) form with only XOR/MAJ logic (no carry propagation). A final `sum + carry` add only at the column bottom. This cut delay from ~820ps to ~669ps (4.62M→2.88M).

2. **Narrow internal accumulator (32→19 bits)** — reduced all psum registers and adders. Max partial sum = 8 × 128² = 2¹⁷, requiring 19-bit signed. This alone took the baseline from 4.62M to 3.24M.

3. **Split CSA into lower 16-bit and upper 3-bit sections** — since the product is 16-bit sign-extended, the upper 3 bits of the CSA operate on identical (sign) bits, simplifying logic. Squeezed 2.88M→2.84M.

4. **Removing resets from all registers except vpipe** — small area saving (3.24M→3.18M on the ripple version).

### What Didn't Work
- **18-bit accumulator** — overflowed (2¹⁷ = 131072 > 2¹⁷-1)
- **Hybrid CSA+ripple (4 or 6 CSA rows then ripple)** — the conversion adder created a longer critical path than full CSA
- **Narrow carry registers (17-18 bits)** — broke correctness due to carry bit 18 being non-zero
- **18-bit deskew registers** — overflowed at 2¹⁷
- **Sign-extension multiplier trick** — no improvement over plain `$signed() * $signed()`
- **Flat packed shift registers** — no measurable benefit
- **Higher target_delay** — consistently worse; delay increased more than area decreased

### Lessons Learned
- The carry-save accumulator is the key technique for systolic MAC chains — it eliminates the cascaded carry propagation that dominates delay.
- Width optimization (finding the minimum safe accumulator width) is a free win with no correctness risk if the range analysis is correct.
- The synthesis tool's default `target_delay=500` was optimal for this design; sweeping it was unproductive.
- Splitting datapaths by bit significance (16-bit product vs 3-bit sign extension) can yield small but real improvements in both area and delay.
